# L1 BeH2 Motif Analysis

This report buckets Level-1 BeH2 episodes by first-hit error rounded to `2` decimals in mHa.

## Scan Scope

- Run directories analyzed: `20`
- Default scope: original 20 completed Level-1 depth-scan runs
- Bucket rule: `failure` if the episode never reaches chemical accuracy; otherwise `round(first_hit_error_mHa, 2)`
